## 11. Review outcome (Draft 0.1 → 0.2)

*Draft 0.2's review record, kept as written; items 2, 4, 6 and q13 are superseded by §12 (the K primitives they said were missing have landed).*

**One adversarial review, 2026-09-03, five passes: consistency, dependencies and ordering, buildability, verify axes, and the §9 defaults.** Every `file:line` in Draft 0.1 was opened in the tree it names — main at `3967e12` (`git show`, not the working tree, because E5b is in flight) and K at `1412f96`. Findings are folded above; nothing is left as a note.

### 11.1 What was wrong and is now changed

**HIGH.**

1. **The corridor had no sale path.** §1.1, §3 and D-E7-26 said the loop runs on "direct vendor sale (E6b's vendor-from-stock path)". No such E6b path exists: `Supply::Stock { vault }`, quotes and vendors are all **E6c's** (08f §2's cut table, §5.2 *"Listings, quotes, vendors"*), so the physical loop had nothing to sell into from cut 3 to cut 11. 10a §4 dep 1 made the error first and Draft 0.1 inherited it. Replaced by **D-E7-31**: a plain two-party E4b2 contract with a funded NPC `Organization`, settling on a new `inventory.transferred` fact — which is exactly what 08f §5.2 says accepting a quote *drafts*, so E7 needs the landed half and none of E6c. Cut 11 no longer depends on E6c.
2. **"No E7 cut blocks on a K item" was false, and its supporting citation was wrong.** Cut 5 needs K5a's one-grant-per-station projection *and* an epoch comparison. `mush-sim/src/data.rs:635-640` — cited by both Draft 0.1 and 10a as "the sim-side staleness check is already the control epoch" — is the **read accessor** `control_generation`; **no epoch comparison exists anywhere in `crates/*/src`** (G4). The rule is restated as "no cut blocks on an *unlanded* K item", and Draft 0.1's q13 is resolved (§11.3) so the merge is a precondition of cut 1 rather than a dependency of cut 5.
3. **`consent_hash` cannot be "shared" as Draft 0.1 described.** Its signature is `consent_hash(issuer: &PrincipalId, consent: &ConsentSurface)` (`contract.rs:1014`), so `mush-campaign` could only call it by stuffing seat and operation fields into an economy `ConsentSurface`. **D-E7-32** factors the canonical-encode-and-digest tail into `consent_digest(domain, canonical)`, makes `consent_hash` its wrapper, and gives cuts 9 and 10 their own domain tags — one implementation, domain-separated, which forking would not have achieved either.
4. **The K6 gate was a promise, not a predicate.** Cut 10 said the live handoff is "stubbed behind the K6 gate" without saying what the stub refuses, leaving the dangerous case open: an NPC automation fallback holding movement and weapons channels *is* an active lease. §3.10 now states three conjunctive conditions for `Dormant → Active` and what relaxes when K6 lands.

**MEDIUM.**

5. **`1A`, `3A`, `4A`, `6A` and `7A` each named two different decisions** — doc 10 §12's survey and 10a's answers both number 1–7. §1 used `3A` for the recovery floor while §2.4 used it for the C8 interim, in one document that calls both binding. Every occurrence is now prefixed `S…`/`Q…` (header notation block).
6. **The `brace` provider does not prove what cut 8 said it proves.** §2.5 and §3.8 claimed "guard revalidation (`module.rs:539-542`)"; those lines are `validate_invocation` delegating to `Self::validate`, and `mush-tactics/src/module.rs` contains no `guard`, `Guard` or `revalidat` token at all. Worse, **no production module in either tree registers an `AuthorityGuardKind` validator** — the only caller of `ModuleRegistry::authority_guard_validator` anywhere is `mush-core/tests/module_api.rs:909` (G3). Cuts 2, 4 and 8 are told they are first, with the real registration verb and the `(OwnerId, kind)` key that makes two owners of one kind safe.
7. **The pulse driver was not specified enough to build.** `ActionInvocation` requires a principal, an actor `ObjId`, an offer id, a subject and a scoped key (`action.rs:687-698`) and a domain tick has no player. §3.8 now names the shape — `PrincipalId::Scoped`, the domain host as actor, `IdempotencyKey::Deterministic` over `(domain, tick)` — and corrects the `PrincipalId` variant list (core has only `Object` and `Scoped`; `System` is `mush_org`'s type).
8. **The bundle-cap claim was too broad.** Only three of the six caps can bind a tick; `MAX_DURABLE_OUTBOX_INTENTS` bounds what a bundle *emits*, and Draft 0.1 derived `MAX_INTENTS_PER_TICK` from it. At 64 units the write count is ~6 % of `MAX_AUTHORITATIVE_REPLAY_WRITES`, so `MAX_UNITS_PER_DOMAIN` is a **latency policy** figure, not a K3-derived one (§2.3, §3.8, §7, q6).
9. **The fronts catch-up row was contradicted by the lines it cited.** `mush-faction`'s `Clock` is a **segment counter** — `segments`/`filled`, advanced by an `advance_on` canon kind — with no `due`, no `ClockStamp` and no deadline (`sim.rs:67-98`). "Restamp preserving the real due instant — a front clock is real-time pressure" restamps something that does not exist. The row now says *no restamp*, and moves the real-deadline case to cut 9's operation window, where 24–72 h of defender notice genuinely is real-world time.
10. **`Resolving`'s acknowledgement set was never enumerated**, so `Resolved` was unreachable by construction. §3.9 makes `ConsumerId` a closed enum of five, states which are conditional on the frozen terms, fixes the required set at entry to `Resolving`, and adds the case Draft 0.1 missed: a later build adding a consumer must not re-open a `Resolved` operation, and an unrecognised stored id pages rather than being dropped.
11. **The E5a `TryFrom` wire rule appeared in one cut and belongs in all eleven** — a derived `Deserialize` makes every §7 cap advisory on load (08d §3.6 A2/A3). Now §4 rule 1 and **D-E7-33**, with boot rules, idempotency-key shapes, projection parity and the LLM rule beside it as §4 rules 2–5.
12. **Two proposal requirements were dropped silently**: line/panel projection parity from one internal view, and the LLM boundary. Both are now explicit — the rule kept in §4, the TacSim panel deliberately out in §1.1.
13. **`mush-inventory` and `mush-industry` had no fact namespace**, so cut 3's extraction and cut 4's completion could not satisfy any contract condition. D-E7-30 goes from three namespaces to five and §10 adds four fact kinds.

**LOW — citations opened and corrected.** `08c:1802-1815` → the K3 review is `1793-1813` with the gates at `:1804-1805`; `08c:1810-1813` → the owner-decision sentence is `:1806-1808` (`1810-1813` is the separate E0-hazard note). `09:2126-2134` → K3 exit criteria are `2122-2133`; `09:2075-2083` → K3.1e is `2071-2078`; `09:2137-2147` → K4a is `2140-2145`; `09:2148-2153` → K5a is `2146-2151`; `09:2151-2153` → that sentence is `2151` alone; `09:2056-2058` → `2057-2058`; `09:989-993` → `989-992`; `09:997-1008` → `997-1006`. K `clock.rs:180-190` is `ic_at`, the evaluator, not the anchoring rule (that is `:161-164` and `next_rate_epoch` `:290-295`); `clock.rs:66-103` → `64-103`; `clock.rs:141-157` is **two** error types (`141-146`, `148-157`); `world_ic_now` is `:482-484`. A bare `module.rs:607-629` for `with_facts` sat next to a `mush-core/src/module.rs` citation and is `mush-**economy**/src/module.rs:615`. `modules.rs:176-182`'s vocabulary is economy + two org ids at `3967e12`; **housing joins it only in the in-flight E5b tree**, where the fn moves to `:211-218`. `manager.rs:44-46` is the field declaration; the 300 s default is `manager.rs:61-70`. `scene_ic_time` has a `round == 0` guard, so "no floor or clamp" became "no monotonic floor". `mush-sim/src/data.rs:678-690` starts mid-doc; `is_sim_bound` is `:682-690`. `links.rs:39-54` → `:40-54`. `AuthorityGuard::ControlLease` is `:1053-1058`. **`crates/*/src` does contain a `Deposit`-named type** — `contract.rs:567` `DepositRecord`, E4b2 escrow — so §2.5's "no deposit type" is corrected and cut 3 is warned off the collision.

### 11.2 What the review confirmed sound

G1 is exactly right and was re-checked across the whole workspace: **no route definition type exists** — only `Leg`, `JourneyStatus`, `Journey` and `RouteState`, with a route parsed per invocation from `<room>[:secs]` tokens — so cut 1's `RouteDef` and D-E7-29 stand. Cut 2's `InventoryReservation` matches doc 09 C8's record **field for field and in order** (`09:997-1006`). K5a is as described: `links.rs:18-59` *(K)* builds one grant per seat from that station's own `StationSpec`, and the word "union" does not occur anywhere in `crates/mush-sim/src/` on the K side. `channel_grant_is_current` fails closed on absence, revocation, epoch bump and narrowed channels in one expression (`world.rs:697-698`). The K3 bundle caps are exact (`authoritative.rs:27-38`). `advance_cadence_manual` has **no sim caller** — only its re-export and one E4a test — so cut 6 really is first. `TacticalUnit`, `Front`/`ControlKind`/`ControlRecord`, `Encumbrance`, `InstrumentKind`, `CadenceClock`, `ConsentSurface`, `ContractRoles`, `EconomicVisibility::Audience`, `create_system_obligation`, `issue_title`, `AccountRec` and the custody trait all verified to the line. Cut 10's `SeatOffer`/`SeatGrant` genuinely name **neither an account nor the absent character** — the only link is the opaque `DriverToken` — and D-E7-2's "one shape, two owners" is not merely defensible but **required by T4 in terms**, which is why it left §9.

### 11.3 What is left for the owner

Ten questions in §9, four of them ★-marked as decisions a default should not make: **q7** (pulling E6a's `mush-party` cut into E7 moves scope between programmes), **q11** (who ships the scene IC time floor — the shape is fixed, the owner is not), **q12** (whether the corridor waits for E6c's vendor path rather than selling by contract), and **q13** (whether cut 10 is worth building before K6, given how narrow the pre-K6 activation predicate is). The other six — the domain limit, tick rates, the digest algorithm, the no-interim rule, where the account seam ships, how the unit cap is measured, the restamp registry's home, the two-crate split and the commodity ids — are ordinary build defaults and are taken as written.

**Three of Draft 0.1's questions are closed rather than carried.** Old **q5** (which crate owns the matter reservation) was never open: doc 10 T4 says "factory and drydock capacity use industry-owned reservations; cargo, components and commodities use inventory/custody reservations". Old **q11** is now ★-11, sharpened into a named-owner default because its own precedent (08f §11 q2) resolved the analogous question by naming E5b, not by waiting. Old **q13** is resolved as instructed: **the K branch merges into main once E5b commits, and E7 develops on main** — `git merge-tree --write-tree 3967e12 1412f96` from base `b34137a` is conflict-free, and developing on K would have forked E5b, E6 and E7 across two branches, with two diverging copies of doc 09, for the length of an eleven-cut build. *(That `merge-tree` check was run for the E5b→K merge and is the only one on record. The later K7/K8 convergence merge at `4c80014` had none — 08j S12; the per-slice branching with a pre-merge `merge-tree` asserted in the headnote and in 08i answer 14 was the plan, not the practice.)*

## 12. Changelog — Draft 0.2 → 0.3 (2026-09-04)

**Cause.** The E/K bilateral review (08i, Draft 0.1 read at `91208ab`) found that six of Draft 0.2's "K has not landed" premises were stale — the merge Draft 0.2 waited on had landed at `1d5aa3d` — and the K side's answers to 08i §7 Q1–Q16, with the owner's rulings (08i §9), amended cuts 5, 6, 7, 8 and 10. Nothing here changes a 10a answer or a doc 10 decision; every change is a re-grounding on delivered K API or the adoption of a ruling.

1. **Header and headnote.** Draft 0.3; "two trees" → one tree at `91208ab`, every `file:line` marked historical, citations by symbol; the merge precondition discharged; K7 on `codex/` branches, E on `main` (answer 14); the three narrow K waits named (K8.0, K7.0–K7.3 for LLM rows, G-B for hooks).
2. **Stale premises struck, by symbol** (08i §2 rows 1–17): "no epoch comparison in main" (G4) → `World::channel_grant_is_current`; "main still unions" → `desired_links`/`refresh_actor_links`; "K5 has not landed" → `OfferRouteTable`/`ProjectedLegalAction`/`Engine::resolve_legal_action`; "K6 remains K6 work" → `ControlLeaseState`/`crates/mush-sim/src/handoff.rs` (a private module; `HandoffProvider`/`HandoffScheduleAdapter` are not re-exported)/`ControlLeaseGuardValidator`; "`ContactId` has no producer" → `ContactState`/`ContactProvider`; "the K lane has no actorless entry" → `scheduled_action_ingress`/`DurableScheduleDriver`; "nothing registers an `AuthorityGuardKind` validator" (G3) → narrowed to the `Reservation` kind; "gate 2 open" → `Ctx::action_state`; plus `WorldTimeline`/`Ctx::world_ic_now`, `ClockProviderRegistry`, `ReservationRecord`/`ReservationBook`, `ActionWindowRecord`, `ResolutionRecord` cited where each cut uses them (§1.1, §2.1–§2.5, §3 table and dependencies, D-E7-11/16/27).
3. **Cut 5** (answer 10; 08i §3 row 2): focus is world-owned ephemeral state keyed by the immutable account id, cleared at boot, reconnect re-claims; no boot-hydrated focus map, no `AccountRec` field, no account-keyed lease; station authority is K6's `(subject, channel)` lease; a focus change revokes/bumps the grant witnesses; `FocusRelease` survives only as `ControlHandoffDraft.reason`; cut 5 drops its release/handoff code and becomes the campaign ingress; the fallback is an immediate deterministic controller under K7.0's rule (answer 3). D-E7-10 amended; D-E7-34 added.
4. **Cut 6** (answers 4, 8, 9): owns the typed `PlacementRecord` migration on K's `revision_validator` contract; `HostStations`/`StationAssignment`/`HostAssignments` are control-topology records, not placement; `DomainRecord` authority is cut 8's; `@domain/promote` never mutates the clock registry; E6b owns the scene IC floor and its monotonicity test, cut 6 the arrival raise. D-E7-35 added.
5. **Cut 7** (answer 1, overriding 08i §6.1): `ContactRecord` removed as a lifecycle record; `ObservationIntent`/`ObservationOp` through the trusted coordinator ingress into `mush-tactics`' `ContactState`; false contacts as propagation-owned `SubjectKey::Scoped` phantoms; truthless contacts K's v2; provenance/confidence a side projection with no lifecycle authority; `MAX_OBSERVATIONS_PER_PASS`. D-E7-15 amended; D-E7-36 added.
6. **Cut 8** (answers 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 11, 15; G-A): the driver implements the owner-scoped `DomainClockProvider` (one per owner, by `domain_id` and generation); the tick enters through `scheduled_action_ingress` as a `Registrar::schedule_source` adapter (K7.2 leaves the draft, principal class and coordinator path unchanged); the frozen set is an `ActionWindowRecord` (`MAX_WINDOW_CAPACITY` = `MAX_INTENTS_PER_TICK`); the production unit family is `TacticalUnit` only and `unit.status` is a projection; `DomainRecord` authority here; controllers select from `ProjectedLegalAction`s — one offer per action kind, ≤ 256 opaque target choices, ranking or sectors above 64 units; the bundle is measured against 1,024 writes / 16 MiB; the `ControlLease` validator is K's (registered by `mush-sim`), not tactics'; RNG through `PrepareCtx::start_resolution`; immediate deterministic fallback per K7.0, LLM rows K7-gated. D-E7-16 amended; D-E7-37/38/39 added.
7. **Cut 10** (answers 2, 3): the K6 gate is a predicate over the K6 memo's production rows (leases, contacts) plus cuts 6/8's migrations, not over an absent kernel; activation is a `control_handoff_request` at a boundary; the no-eviction policy retained; fixture-only rows off the path; LLM-driven seats K7-gated. D-E7-21 amended; D-E7-42 added; §9 q13 closed.
8. **§4.1 "Rule 1 categories by cut"** (answer 12): the narrower exception ratified; a category per record per cut; the pinned allowlist tripwire in `mush-contract-tests`, extended by every cut in the same commit; the economy carve-out only where the Ledger/seal gates bind. D-E7-40 added.
9. **§5 E7k** (answer 13; G-B): gates 1 and 3 are K8.0's; gate 2 closed except the hook-read context, a K item E7's hooks wait on; E7k after K8.0 and K8.1–K8.3. D-E7-41 added.
10. **§4 build discipline:** confirmation by symbol; fmt-clean per cut (`91208ab`); the "sixth item" sentence re-grounded; rule 5 states the K7 gate.
11. **§7 / §9:** `MAX_INTENTS_PER_TICK` = `MAX_WINDOW_CAPACITY`; `MAX_OBSERVATIONS_PER_PASS`; q6 records lane occupancy (one unresolved authoritative intent per store/backend, `PendingDecision`-only ceiling, slot hold time measured beside bundle size); q11 closed to E6b; q13 closed.
12. **Not changed, deliberately:** every 10a answer (§8), the eleven-cut order, the corridor's contract sale (D-E7-31), `consent_digest` (D-E7-32), the constants other than the two noted, and the *(main)*/*(K)* line-number citations Draft 0.2 verified — they are kept as historical evidence and marked so, not re-verified against the reformatted tree (08i §8's rule: symbols, not lines).
